<div class="pane-content">
<h2>It Pays to Visit</h2>
<p class="lead">We’ll help you get here and give you $4,000 toward tuition!</p>
<p>We’re serious. Come check out our Crete campus and we’ll help pay for your travel and give you $1,000 a year toward tuition. It’s that simple. Highlights of your visit include:</p>
<ul><li>A personalized tour with a current student.</li>
<li>Meet with a professor.</li>
<li>Check out the housing, dining, athletic and program facilities.</li>
<li>Discover what makes Doane unique.</li>
</ul><a class="btn--hollow" href="/visit/">Schedule a Visit</a> </div>

<h2 class="block-title"><a href="http://doane.edu/news">News »</a></h2>
<div class="content clearfix">
<div class="view view-news view-id-news view-display-id-fp_news_feed fp-news view-dom-id-e2fed33fb4ab2bb39cc9151c49009297 jquery-once-1-processed">
<div class="view-content">
<div class="fp-news-story">
<p class="fp-news-date">October 18, 2019</p>
<div class="fp-news-thumbnail"><a href="/news/a-leader-on-and-off-the-field-yadav-20-serves-as-chair-of-naia-association-of-student-athletes" title=" Link"><img alt="Nishesh Yadav ’20" height="427" src="https://www.doane.edu/sites/default/files/styles/news_block_front_page/public/DIVAS/Nish%20Yadav%2019%20web.jpg?itok=dVRfVVV3" typeof="foaf:Image" width="640"/></a></div>
<h4 class="fp-news-title"><a href="/news/a-leader-on-and-off-the-field-yadav-20-serves-as-chair-of-naia-association-of-student-athletes" title="A leader on and off the field, Yadav ’20 serves as chair of NAIA Association of Student-Athletes Link">A leader on and off the field, Yadav ’20 serves as chair of NAIA Association of Student-Athletes</a></h4>
<p class="fp-news-summary"> Nishesh Yadav ’20, a senior on Doane men’s soccer team, is serving as the Chair of the NAIA Association of Student-Athletes this academic year. In this role Yadav acts as the voice of all 65,000 NAIA student-athletes, bridging the gap between NAIA administration and its student-athletes. 
 </p>
<span class="fp-news-moreLink"><a href="/news/a-leader-on-and-off-the-field-yadav-20-serves-as-chair-of-naia-association-of-student-athletes" title="More &amp;raquo; Link">More »</a></span> </div>
<div class="fp-news-story">
<p class="fp-news-date">October 16, 2019</p>
<div class="fp-news-thumbnail"><a href="/news/50-years-after-sports-illustrated-feature-doane-football-players-reunite-at-homecoming" title=" Link"><img alt="Members of Doane football teams from 1967-1970" height="427" src="https://www.doane.edu/sites/default/files/styles/news_block_front_page/public/DIVAS/Undefeated%20football%20teams%20web.jpg?itok=ahyeXeWw" typeof="foaf:Image" width="640"/></a></div>
<h4 class="fp-news-title"><a href="/news/50-years-after-sports-illustrated-feature-doane-football-players-reunite-at-homecoming" title="50 years after Sports Illustrated feature, Doane football players reunite at Homecoming Link">50 years after Sports Illustrated feature, Doane football players reunite at Homecoming</a></h4>
<p class="fp-news-summary"> In October of 1969, Sports Illustrated wrote a two-page feature story on Doane's football team, which at the time, held the nation's longest winning streak in college football. Fifty years later, players from those undefeated teams reunited at Doane for Homecoming week. 
 </p>
<span class="fp-news-moreLink"><a href="/news/50-years-after-sports-illustrated-feature-doane-football-players-reunite-at-homecoming" title="More &amp;raquo; Link">More »</a></span> </div>
</div>
</div> </div>
</div>
</div>
